petitioners, Sri. M.Vinodkumar, learned AGA for respondent
Nos.1, 2 and 6 and Shri G.V.Shashi Kumar, learned
advocate for respondent No.4.

submits that elections have already been conducted.
Therefore, this petition is rendered infructuous and
accordingly prayed for its dismissal. He has filed a memo of
even date to the said effect. Memo be kept in record.
3. In the circumstance, petition is dismissed as
having become infructuous.
